## 2. Exact transformation algebra

Each record r freezes an original half-open interval [O,O+P_bytes) in B. P is the
exact preimage bytes shown as lowercase hex. Q is the exact postimage bytes shown
as lowercase hex. Records are applied simultaneously, equivalently by copying
untouched B spans in ascending O order or by applying them in descending O order.
All record intervals are pairwise disjoint. An anchor is the shortest recorded
bounded neighbor expansion used by the mechanical authoring scan whose complete
bytes occur exactly once in B. Global P occurrence count is reported separately;
it is not confused with anchor uniqueness. No locator ordinal or author choice is
left for a materializer.

I is the exact result. Its complete identity and all arithmetic are frozen below.
No output path follows from this identity. Any later materialization would require
a new ledger event and a newly created disposable directory strictly below
/tmp/p27-e001-synthetic-supervisor after proving exclusion from the repository,
all build/evidence trees, every control tree, and every old object.

A|    e001Trace("context-" + context)
A|    return context
A|def e001ExitTransition(code):
A|    spec = e001Spec()
A|    need(type(code) is int and code in (0, 1), "E001_SYNTHETIC_EXIT_STATUS")
A|    need(E001SyntheticState["exit_records"] == [], "E001_SYNTHETIC_EXIT_REPEAT")
A|    record = (
A|        "E001-SYNTHETIC-EXIT-V6",
A|        spec["run_id"],
A|        spec["mode"],
A|        code,
A|        E001SyntheticState["invocation_capability"],
A|    )
A|    E001SyntheticState["exit_records"].append(record)
A|    E001SyntheticHost["exit_records"].append(record)
A|    return record
A|def e001DriverSelect():
A|    original = tuple(sys.argv)
A|    need(len(original) >= 2, "E001_DRIVER_ARGV_SHORT")
A|    scenario_matches = tuple(row for row in E001ScenarioTable if row[0] == original[-1])
A|    need(len(scenario_matches) == 1, "E001_DRIVER_SCENARIO_CARDINALITY")
A|    scenario = scenario_matches[0]
A|    runs = tuple(sorted(
A|        (row for row in E001RunTable if row[1] == scenario[0]),
A|        key=lambda row: row[2],
A|    ))
A|    modes = E001ModeSequence.get(scenario[0])
A|    need(
A|        E001SyntheticHost["driver_scenario"] is None
A|        and len(runs) == len(modes)
A|        and tuple(row[2] for row in runs) == tuple(range(len(runs)))
A|        and tuple(row[4] for row in runs) == modes
A|        and all(row[3] == len(runs) for row in runs)
A|        and original == e001ExpectedArgv(runs[0][4], scenario[0]),
A|        "E001_DRIVER_SELECTION",
A|    )
A|    E001SyntheticHost["driver_scenario"] = scenario[0]
A|    return scenario, runs
A|def e001BeginInvocation(scenario, run):
A|    global E001SyntheticState
A|    E001SyntheticState = e001NewSyntheticState()
A|    e001PrimeInvocation(scenario, run, True)
A|    contract = tuple(row for row in E001ObserverContractTable if row[0] == run[0])
A|    need(len(contract) <= 1, "E001_DRIVER_OBSERVER_CARDINALITY")
A|    return None if not contract else e001FreezeObserverExpectation(run[0])
A|def e001RunCopiedInvocation():
A|    mode = E001SyntheticState["mode"]
A|    binding = Binding(sys.argv[2:13])
A|    if mode == "supervisor":
A|        return supervisor_mode(binding)
A|    if mode == "watchdog":
A|        return watchdog_mode(binding)
A|    if mode == "recovery-binder":
A|        return recovery_mode(binding)
A|    raise RuntimeError("E001_DRIVER_MODE")
A|def e001CaptureInvocationReturn(result):
A|    spec = e001Spec()
A|    need(
A|        isinstance(result, tuple)
A|        and len(result) == 5
A|        and result[0] == "E001-SYNTHETIC-EXIT-V6"
A|        and result[1] == spec["run_id"]
A|        and result[2] == spec["mode"]
A|        and type(result[3]) is int
A|        and result[3] in (0, 1)
A|        and result[4] is E001SyntheticState["invocation_capability"]
A|        and E001SyntheticState["exit_records"] == [result],
A|        "E001_DRIVER_EXIT_CAPTURE",
A|    )
A|    ordered = tuple(E001SyntheticState["ordered_trace"])
A|    driver_record = (
A|        spec["run_id"],
A|        spec["mode"],
A|        result[3],
A|        result[4],
A|        ordered,
A|    )
A|    E001SyntheticHost["driver_runs"].append(driver_record)
A|    host_run = E001SyntheticHost["observer_runs"].get(spec["run_id"])
A|    if host_run is None:
A|        need(ordered == E001TracePlans[spec["run_id"]], "E001_DRIVER_TRACE_INCOMPLETE")
A|    else:
A|        need(host_run["trace"] == [], "E001_DRIVER_TRACE_ALREADY_CAPTURED")
A|        host_run["trace"] = list(ordered)
A|    return result[3]
A|def e001DriveObserver(expectation):
A|    run_id = e001Spec()["run_id"]
A|    need(expectation is not None, "E001_DRIVER_OBSERVER_EXPECTATION")
A|    namespace_handle = e001ObserverNamespaceOpen(run_id)
A|    e001ObserverProducerReturned(
A|        namespace_handle,
A|        E001ReportDestinationBasename,
A|        expectation,
A|    )
A|    e001ObserverEnter(
A|        namespace_handle,
A|        E001ReportDestinationBasename,
A|        expectation,
A|    )
A|    host_run = E001SyntheticHost["observer_runs"][run_id]
A|    need(
A|        tuple(host_run["trace"]) == E001TracePlans[run_id]
A|        and len(host_run["external_observations"]) == 1,
A|        "E001_DRIVER_OBSERVER_TRACE_INCOMPLETE",
A|    )
A|def e001DriveScenario(scenario, runs):
A|    if scenario[0] == "S12":
A|        need(len(runs) == 2, "E001_DRIVER_S12_RUNS")
A|        expectation = e001BeginInvocation(scenario, runs[0])
A|        need(expectation is None, "E001_DRIVER_S12_J0_OBSERVER")
A|        producer_result = e001RunCopiedInvocation()
A|        e001CaptureInvocationReturn(producer_result)
A|        transport = E001SyntheticHost["transport"]
A|        e001TransportProducerReturned(transport)
A|        e001TransportCarry(transport)
A|        e001TransportEnterConsumer(transport, scenario, runs[1])
A|        expectation = e001FreezeObserverExpectation(runs[1][0])
A|        consumer_result = e001RunCopiedInvocation()
A|        e001CaptureInvocationReturn(consumer_result)
A|        e001DriveObserver(expectation)
A|        return
A|    need(len(runs) == 1, "E001_DRIVER_SINGLE_RUN")
A|    expectation = e001BeginInvocation(scenario, runs[0])
A|    result = e001RunCopiedInvocation()
A|    e001CaptureInvocationReturn(result)
A|    if expectation is not None:
A|        e001DriveObserver(expectation)
A|def e001SyntheticDriver():
A|    scenario, runs = e001DriverSelect()
A|    e001DriveScenario(scenario, runs)
A|    need(
A|        tuple(row[0] for row in E001SyntheticHost["driver_runs"])
A|        == tuple(row[0] for row in runs),
A|        "E001_DRIVER_RUN_COVERAGE",
A|    )
A|    return tuple(
A|        (row[0], row[1], row[2]) for row in E001SyntheticHost["driver_runs"]
A|    )
A|# E001_SYNTHETIC_ADAPTER_V6_END

| `e001SyntheticDriver(` | 2 | declaration plus exact callers |

Thus no real exit or unredirected path, raw-I/O, process, signal, wait, clock
or sleep atom remains. `e001ExitTransition(` occurs once in its declaration
and eleven times at copied terminals; `e001SyntheticDriver(` occurs once in
its declaration and once at the sole constructed-I module entry.
